a team's path / target test uses its first live member; the "flagged path" refusal is the host's `movement_locked` query (it also covers the script lock); the coordinator move is a `Lured` event the host turns into an attack-move toward the lure (goal = the lure, or its team); after the last pulse the behaviour suspends but the ability slot runs on to Expiration_Seconds [M]; (cu8g t05) an effect requested and not yet bound when the lure is deactivated (or its owner removed) in the same tick is marked cancelled: `bind_spawn` refuses it and the host removes the new object silently (it keeps the object id the original's create-then-kill takes); `Is_Pulsing` stays parsed and unread [M]. **Max_Num_Spawned_Objects**: an absent tag is -1 = no cap (the Unit_Ability ctor default was not read [L]); a squad = one DEPLOY_TROOPERS spawn, live while one of its bound troopers is still a combat unit or one of its spawn requests is unbound. **Enable_* lists**: tactical bool lists 4..6 in the player-entry list (no save layout change); the Lua `Disable_Bombing_Run` / `Disable_Orbital_Bombardment` entry has the fixed key 0x407ff8a8 and no source, and the old record `disabled` byte is still ANDed (older saves); Reverse_Application_Logic swaps apply / remove for every list of the modifier (cu8g t05: the reveal list and the float lists too, `AbilitySystem::modifier_entries`; the earlier "reverse = enemies [M]" reading is gone, so the comm array's Slower_Reinforcements now slows its owner and allies once the array is removed, not the enemies while it stands). **Super_Laser**: the Face_Target turn runs in the Super_Laser update (the TSW service is not modelled) and goes through the host's `snap_yaw`; Fire_Continuation starts when the update finds the target dead (the original's vf13 trigger [M]). **INFECTION**: (cu8g t05) a parameterless infection (flag only: spawn / Lua, no infector pack) is serviced with frames left 0 (the pack ctor value [L] taken as 0): the frame % 60 draw as usual, then it suspends at its first service (no damage, no spread); the spread candidate test reads obj +0x2a0 = the object's model (MOVEMENT / SPACE_AVOIDANCE: never made when the type names no model for the mode) as `UnitDesc::has_model` = the type names a model for the battle's mode (the file is not checked; true without model data); spread order = player index, then the per-player units in ascending id; the per-unit walk is the combat system's (descending slot), so a unit infected this frame may already have had its service. Saves: ability save v4 and combat save v13 (shared with cu8f t01 / t04; only the current version is read, cu6).
- **BM2a · alamo_game replay support and benchmark mode (2026-11-06, roadmap group bm2a).**
  **t01: what alamo_game can replay today** (so BM2 can load BM1 scenarios). (1) **Save load: yes.** Engine-format saves
  (`.alsv`, `save_games.hpp`; `GameSession::start_from_save`, `load_game(data, js, path, &err, replay, record)`) load
  through the Load dialog and, since bm2a, from the command line (`--bench-save FILE`, `App::load_save_file`). A save taken
  during a battle comes back in it (its load screen first); a save of other game data / options / seed is refused
  (`SessionDescriptor::mismatch`). (2) **Recorded orders: recorded, replayed only headless.** Every drained command batch
  goes into a `commands::CommandRecording` (`GameSession::record_to`; a loaded session records the stream after the load);
  smoke runs write it as `<png-dir>/game_smoke_<kind>.rec`. A recording is replayed by `GameSession::start(diag, &replay)` /
  `load_game(..., &replay)` (live `submit()` is then ignored by the pipeline), but only by `crc_check()`'s presentation-free
  replay at the end of a smoke or benchmark run: the exe has no flag to play a `.rec` with rendering. BM2's hook is to pass
  the scenario's recording as `load_game`'s `replay` in `bench_start` (one argument; the rest of the benchmark loop is
  unchanged). (3) **CRC output: yes.** The SimHost per-tick `CrcLog` is always on (`session_->host().crc_log()`); smoke
  runs save it as `game_smoke_<kind>.crc` and compare it with the goldens (`compare_smoke.cmake`), benchmark runs with
  `--bench-crc-out FILE` (full ALAMO_CRC 1 log) and print the final combined CRC in the report. No fingerprint check in
  alamo_game: BM1 defines it in alamo_headless.
  **Benchmark mode (t02-t04)**, `alamo_game --benchmark realtime|fixed --bench-save FILE [--bench-preset NAME]
  [--bench-ticks N] [--bench-frames-per-tick K] [--bench-report FILE] [--bench-crc-out FILE] [--bench-png-dir DIR
  --bench-png-every N] [--bench-settle N] [--bench-max-unsettled N] [--bench-no-verify]`. Entry point for BM2:
  `game::BenchmarkOptions` (benchmark.hpp); the exe reads nothing else for a run. Phases: load the save -> settle (the sim
  holds until `--bench-settle` (3) settled gameplay frames in a row: no pending pipeline / model / texture / terrain work)
  -> measure `--bench-ticks` (900) ticks -> report (stdout JSON, `--bench-report`), CRC log, and unless
  `--bench-no-verify` the presentation on/off check (the save replayed with the run's recorded commands gives the same CRC
  log; exit 1 when it diverges). Real-time: the original pacing (`GameSession::loop_iteration`, FrameSynchronizer 30
  ticks/s on the wall clock, no catch-up), interpolated rendering as in live play; frame time median / p99 / worst
  (nearest rank) over the gameplay frames, plus render and sim (frames that ticked) times. Fixed frames: exactly K counted
  frames per tick (`FixedFrameClock`), the presentation clock = counted frames / (30 K) s, alpha (sub + 1) / K,
  Immediate present mode (V-sync off) and no frame cap whatever the preset says, blocking model loads, the preset's TAA (BM2b; BM2a turned it off); a frame
  counts only when the presentation is settled, else it is drawn again with the same sim state and sub-frame (more than
  `--bench-max-unsettled` (600) in a row fails the run). Frames outside gameplay (load screen, battle-end screen, a dialog
  holding the sim) are excluded in both modes. Disabled in a run (t04): player input (the idle frame: cursor parked, not
  inside the window, no WM_CHAR text), cursor, tooltips (encyclopedia), tactical / galactic hover, the frame-time overlay,
  intro movies, the attract demo, the video options file and display mode changes; audio = null backend; the unsynced
  presentation generator is seeded fixed. What waits for the player is answered in the frame it appears: battle prompt =
  Begin (fight, drawn), battle load screen = Begin, battle-end screen = Continue, story dialog = closed, full-screen movie
  = finished at once (STORY_MOVIE_DONE sent), advisor loop = stopped, story summary = closed, a saved pause = lifted (all
  listed in the report's `autopilot`); a retry box (mission lost) or the session ending (skirmish over, campaign end)
  before the target ticks fails the run. Presentation timing that reaches the sim is tied to ticks so real-time runs keep
  the same CRC: the audio clock advances 1/30 s per tick that ran (STORY_SPEECH_DONE), and every story UI notification
  raised between two ticks is submitted just before the next tick. Only frames that were actually drawn count. Known
  residual risk: presentation state that advances per drawn frame and not per counted frame would break fixed-frames
  identity on a redraw (the galactic view's own particles are stepped with a zero time on a redraw); the tests check
  identical PNGs from a battle save and a galactic save.
  [port] presets (`bench_presets`): `bm-low` 1280x720 detail 0, no TAA / soft shadows / heat / bloom; `bm-medium` 1920x1080
  detail 40, no TAA, 4x anisotropy; `bm-high` (default) 1920x1080 detail 75, TAA Medium (63), 8x; `bm-ultra` 1920x1080
  detail 100, TAA Medium, ambient occlusion, 16x; `bm-smoke` 640x360 detail 75, no TAA (tests). All: V-sync off, no cap,
  gamma 50, null audio; `--width` / `--height` win over the preset's size. **BM2b (2026-11-06):** fixed-frames runs
  keep the preset's TAA. Every measured frame is drawn with `RecordOptions::taa_provisional`; `bench_frame_end` keeps a
  counted frame (`Renderer::keep_taa_frame`) and takes any other one (unsettled, excluded, forced) back out of the TAA
  state (`Renderer::discard_taa_frame`: jitter index, history slot / validity, previous camera and the object-motion records
  return to where the frame started; the GPU history needs nothing, the frame only read the previous slot), and the history
  restarts with the measurement (the warm-up length varies). Test flag `--bench-redraw-every N` (fixed frames): every Nth
  settled measured frame is drawn again as if unsettled; the report adds `forced_redraws` and `taa_discarded`. Normal
  rendering is unchanged (the flag is off outside fixed-frames measuring). Tests: `alamo_game_benchmark_tests` (data-free), and on the FoC
  data `alamo_game_bench_fixed` / `_fixed_galactic` / `_realtime` (on the saveload smoke's saves: two fixed-frames runs
  from the battle save and two from the galactic save draw identical PNGs and write identical CRC logs; two real-time runs from the galactic save report frame times and the same CRC). Left for BM2 (group bm2): the scenario file as
  the source of `BenchmarkOptions` and of the recording, the tick-driven camera path, GPU timer scopes and the image-match
  check.
- 🟡 **PARTLY DONE** · CU8F t03 (2026-11-07, random map picker, `docs/re/CU8F_T03_T05_RE.md` §1): the campaign picker 0x47bf40
  runs on the candidate table of every `.ted` map (`sim::build_map_candidates`, campaign_battles.hpp): list A
  `Data\Art\Maps\*.TED` of the FoC-loose and MEG layers (every readable file), list B `Data\CustomMaps\*.TED` of those layers
  and `Data\Art\Maps\*.TED` of the mod layer (custom flag `0B` = 1 only); fields from chunk 0 (`01` type, `06` terrain, `05`
  faction, `02` players, `03` max base level, `0B` custom) with the retail defaults (type −1, terrain 0, faction 0, players
  1, max base level 1, custom 0). The filter, the preferred-name stop and the store-back (an empty answer too) follow §1.3 /
  §1.4 (galactic `ConflictManager::choose_map`; query faction −1 only for defender id −1). Hosts wired: alamo_game
  (`GameSession`, any session with a VFS), alamo_headless `--ai`, `--battles` (VFS only, not `--data`), save runs.
  [port] choices: (1) the table is built **on the first pick**, not at boot (`CampaignBattleOptions::map_candidate_source`):
  same table (the files do not change in a game) without reading ~330 MB of maps at every session start; (2) table order =
  `Vfs::list`'s sorted logical-path order per listing (retail: the finder's loose-then-archive order); entries are keyed by
  logical path, so a mod copy of an official map is one entry (retail lists it twice under two names, the second only
  when its `0B` = 1, which the campaign never picks); an unreadable file or one without chunk 0 gives no entry (it could
  never match: type −1); (3) **RNG**: retail draws `int(0, n − 1)` on the free, QPC-seeded RNG; the port draws the same
  `int(0, n − 1)` (no draw for n = 1) on a local `SimRng` seeded with FNV-1a over (space, terrain, faction, base level,
  preferred name, n). Neither session stream is used: the synced one is not drawn by retail here, and the port's free
  stream must never feed the sim (sim_rng.hpp) and its draw count depends on the presentation attached (SimAnimation), so
  a replay with and without rendering could pick differently. Retail's choice is noise, so any deterministic choice
  among the survivors is behaviour-equivalent (§1.6). The game type 4 parts (mode tag `CONTROL`, quickmatch exclusion
  list, start-position need) are not modelled: the campaign query never sets them. ⬜ Open: what the loader does with
  the missing fallback maps `_Desert_L5_01.ted` / `_Space_Temperate1.ted` (in neither FoC nor EaW `maps.meg`) is not
  traced (§1.4); the port keeps BT §2.1 steps 1.3–1.5 unchanged (a missing map refuses the battle, which is then
  auto-resolved [port]). Probe §1.7 (mod planet without a tactical map) not run.
- 🟡 **PARTLY DONE** · CU8F t05 (2026-11-07, scene wind, `docs/re/CU8F_T03_T05_RE.md` §2): `render::scene_wind(map)`
  (map_scene.hpp) feeds `ParticleRenderer::set_wind` at map load in alamo_game's battle view and `alamo_render --sim`: land =
  the start environment's `Environment::wind_vector()` (`2C` · (cos w, sin w, 0), w = `2B` degrees), space = (0, 0, 0);
  no per-frame re-apply. The GPU update already matched §2.3 (wind-affected: velocity += wind · 0.5 · dt; weather
  particles step with velocity + 5 · wind). [port] a land map without environments gets the record ctor default (0°,
  2.0). Open items (1) ~~`Set_New_Environment` and the typed pick have no host~~, (2) ~~map-load environment pick not
  ported~~ and (3) ~~weather-particle inherit term~~: all done in cu8g t02 / t03 (below). Env mini `13` = weather type is
  recorded in TERRAIN_WATER_POST §5.2 (`Environment::field_13`); cu8g t03's typed pick reads it.
- 🟡 **PARTLY DONE** · CU8G t02 / t03 (2026-11-08, environment pick and changes, spec `docs/re/CU8G_T02_ENV_PICK_RE.md`):
  **t02** `TacticalBattle::setup` picks the active environment right after `bt_map_load` (chunk-0x102 objects and their
  spawn-table draws) and before every post-load step (pad restore, skirmish base, starbase / structures / start forces):
  `pick_environment` = 0x277030, one synced `int(0, n − 1)` when n ≠ 1, a record named exactly `Cinematic`
  (case-sensitive) -> the next one wrapping to 0, no re-check; land and space (`_MP_SPACE_KUAT` draws once and always
  ends on `Environment 1`). No draw at mode creation. The index (`env_index_`) is **not saved and not hashed** (its only
  logic effect is the land weather restart, visible in the weather state and the RNG on the next land tick); no state
  version bump. The weather (re)start (`WeatherSystem::start`) now runs the start phase's draw **twice** (§4; the second
  timer stays, one `PhaseStarted` event); the advance / `Force_Weather` keeps one. alamo_game's `BattleView` sets the
  battle's index on `MapScene` before `start()`, so lighting, clouds, wind and skydomes are the picked environment's.
  **t03** Lua `Set_New_Environment(int)` checks its LUA_API contract strings, records, and calls
  `TacticalScriptHooks::set_new_environment` (story VMs and AI VMs) -> `TacticalBattle::set_environment` = vf25: clamp to
  [0, n − 1], no draw. Land weather: `l1_weather_tick` compares the active index with the one it started for; a change
  restarts the weather on the next service with the new environment's scenario (2 draws with phases; none / no phases ->
  off and the all-1 accuracy row); the same index re-inits nothing. Space: index only. Render: `MapScene::set_environment`
  after `start()` re-applies lighting (lights, SH, ambient, fog, bloom, shadow colour, clear colour) and on land the clouds
  (new `WorldRenderer::set_clouds`: scale / velocity at once, a different texture uploaded and swapped in once resident);
  the load-time skydomes stay (their lighting fields are kept); `scene_wind()` follows (land; space zero) and
  `BattleView` rewrites `ParticleRenderer::set_wind`. Typed pick (vf27, no caller in retail or the port):
  `set_environment_of_weather_type(type, rng)`: the records whose `13` == type (type ≤ 4), m = 1 -> it, m ≥ 2 -> one synced
  `int(0, m − 1)`, no Cinematic skip; m = 0 / type > 4 -> `pick_environment`. Weather particles (§6): the weather step adds
  `inherit_velocity_factor · E` (emitter velocity) when `inherit_velocity` is set, before the wind term, without changing
  the stored velocity; the spawn-time inherit add is skipped for weather particles (off in all vanilla weather emitters).
  [port] choices: a battle save load takes the map's start index (vf25 clamp, no draw) and keeps the saved weather state
  as bound to it (no restart); retail's forced weather rebinds on load (2 + 2 draws and an advance [M]) are not modelled.
  A map without environments (synthetic) never draws and ignores Set_New_Environment (retail: the default record, n = 1).
  An environment without a cloud texture keeps the drawn one. `alamo_render --sim` has no battle and keeps the map's
  start environment. ⬜ Open: Gabriel's probes P1 (draw order), P2 (save reload shows the start environment), P3 (Kuat)
  of CU8G_T02_ENV_PICK_RE §7.
